- **loki**: Loki is a log aggregation system designed to be cost-effective and easy to operate. It takes a fundamentally different approach from Elasticsearch or Splunk: instead of indexing log content, it indexes only the labels attached to log streams. Use as the variant-specific playbook when the target matches this profile; start at the parent class skill for general methodology. ([W4L33Dx/Cyber-Monster](https://github.com/W4L33Dx/Cyber-Monster/tree/HEAD/red-team/network-ad-and-endpoint/lolbins-and-signed-binary-abuse-execution-proxies) · ★68)
- **lolbins-detect**: Detect abuse of legitimate Windows binaries (LOLBins) used for living off the land attacks. Monitors process creation, command-line arguments, and parent-child relationships to identify suspicious LOLBin execution patterns. ([DCx7C5/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/DCx7C5/ai-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/windows/lolbins/detect))
- **lolbins-hunt**: Detect Living Off the Land Binaries (LOLBins/LOLBAS) abuse including certutil, regsvr32, mshta, and rundll32 via process telemetry, Sigma rules, and parent-child process analysis ([DCx7C5/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/DCx7C5/ai-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/windows/lolbins/hunt))
- **lolcat**: Use this skill when the user wants to use the lolcat command-line tool. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/lolcat/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **lolcatjs**: Rainbow colorized output. Use when installing or running lolcatjs (lolcatjs). ([RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-cli](https://github.com/RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-cli/tree/HEAD/plugins/lolcatjs/skills/lolcatjs))

- **lolcoach-agents**: Vendor-neutral context and rules for AI assistants working on the LoL Coach repository. ([NexusHero/LoLCoach](https://github.com/NexusHero/LoLCoach/tree/HEAD/skills/lolcoach-agents) · ★3)
- **lolcoach-claude**: Claude-specific guidance for the LoL Coach repository. ([NexusHero/LoLCoach](https://github.com/NexusHero/LoLCoach/tree/HEAD/skills/lolcoach-claude) · ★3)

- **lolimom-companion**: Build or roleplay a cute anime-style mother companion from user notes, dialogue samples, tone preferences, memory lists, and favorite character references. Use when asked to create, refine, document, or chat with a soft, playful, caring fictional mother persona that comforts, reminds, teases lightly, and keeps track of everyday habits in a kawaii anime voice. ([yuuiwa1551/lolimom.skill](https://github.com/yuuiwa1551/lolimom.skill/tree/HEAD/lolimom-companion) · ★4)
- **lolipop-deploy**: ロリポップ！レンタルサーバーにWebサイトやアプリをSSH経由で自動デプロイする。セットアップからアプリ作成、rsyncデプロイまでを対話的にガイドする。「ロリポップにデプロイ」「lolipopにサイトを公開」「ロリポップでサイトを作りたい」「レンタルサーバーにデプロイ」「lolipop deploy」「ロリポップにアップロード」「ロリポップでWebサイト公開」などロリポップ！へのサイト公開に関する話題が出たら、このスキルを参照すること。 ([pepabo/pepabo-marketplace](https://github.com/pepabo/pepabo-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/lolipop-deploy/skills/lolipop-deploy) · ★1)
- **lollapalooza**: Apply Charlie Munger's latticework of mental models to a decision or problem — route to the plugin's existing specialist agents as lenses where relevant, apply inversion/incentive-analysis/base-rate checks inline, and synthesize on convergence (multiple independent lenses agreeing is a stronger signal than any one alone) versus tension (a genuinely hard trade-off). Use for a decision or problem worth more than one perspective, not a trivial or already-obvious call. ([bernardoforcillo/skills](https://github.com/bernardoforcillo/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/skills/skills/lollapalooza) · 2 copies)
- **lollapalooza-check**: Detect when several ordinary psychological tendencies combine to produce an extreme and irrational result. Use when: Use before a high-pressure sale, crowd-driven decision, escalation of commitment, charismatic pitch, crisis response, or unanimous meeting. ([adamtpang/summon.guide](https://github.com/adamtpang/summon.guide/tree/HEAD/plugins/charlie-munger/skills/lollapalooza-check))
- **Lollapalooza效应检测**: 识别多种正向因素叠加产生的超级投资机会 ([louloulin/claude-agent-sdk](https://github.com/louloulin/claude-agent-sdk/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lollapalooza-detection) · ★24 · 2 copies)
- **Lollms Artifacts and Surgical Patching**: Teaches the model how to formulate strategic &lt;coding_plan&gt; blocks, apply indentation-agnostic Aider search/replace patches, manage Git-like version logs, and control in-message status reporting. ([ParisNeo/lollms_client](https://github.com/ParisNeo/lollms_client/tree/HEAD/skills/artifacts_and_patching) · ★13)
- **Lollms Cognitive Persistent Memory**: Teaches the model about the persistent tiered memory system (Level 1 Working, Level 2 Deep, Level 3 Archive), proactive keyword pulling, and the required tagging protocols. ([ParisNeo/lollms_client](https://github.com/ParisNeo/lollms_client/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive_memory) · ★13)
- **Lollms Communication Protocol (LCP) and Local Smart Tools**: Teaches how to build, discover, and execute local, zero-configuration Python tools in Lollms using AST schema parsing and context injection. Includes step-by-step tool file construction guide and code examples. ([ParisNeo/lollms_client](https://github.com/ParisNeo/lollms_client/tree/HEAD/skills/lcp_smart_tools) · ★13)
- **LoLLMS Connection Bindings**: Teaches how to use LoLLMS connection bindings to send messages to communication services (Discord, Telegram, Slack, WhatsApp, webhooks). ([ParisNeo/lollms_client](https://github.com/ParisNeo/lollms_client/tree/HEAD/skills/connection_bindings) · ★13)
- **Lollms Discussion and Chat**: Teaches the model how to manage stateful discussions, track conversational trees/branches, navigate alternative sibling replies, and execute context-aware generation. - **Lollms Multimodal Document and Image Parsing**: Teaches how to import, parse, and process PDF, DOCX, PPTX, and image files using anchor-mapped and OCR import modes in LollmsDiscussion. - **Lollms Swarms and Multi-Agent Orchestration**: Teaches how to coordinate multiple specialized agents, distribute tasks, establish consensus, and manage role-based multi-agent swarms using LollmsSwarm. - **Lollms Text Generation**: Guides the model on performing basic text generation, counting tokens, detokenizing lists, and generating structurally validated JSON outputs with LollmsClient. ([ParisNeo/lollms_client](https://github.com/ParisNeo/lollms_client/tree/HEAD/skills/text_generation) · ★13)
